  [c] Θ(1540) is a hypothetical pentaquark state of 1.54 GeV/c2 mass and a
      width of less than 25 MeV/c2 .
  [d] Includes p p π + π − γ and excludes p p η, p p ω, p p η ′ .
  [e] For a narrow state A with mass less than 960 MeV.
  [f ] For a narrow scalar or pseudoscalar A0 with mass 0.21–3.0 GeV.
  [g ] For a dark photon U with mass between 100 and 2100 MeV.

          χ2 = 215.4 for 96 degrees of freedom.
